For a dense subset A of Z_n of density α, the number of representations of t as a+b is convolution 1_A * 1_A. The Fourier transform: count(t) = α^2 N + ∑_{ξ≠0} |hat A(ξ)|^2 e^{2πi ξ t/N}. Can we exploit FFT for convolution and derivative. Need implement correlate_w_q(w, q): returns d_i =∑_k w_k q_{k-i}. Using FFT. Pseudocode:
```
def deriv_from_w_q(w, q):
    n=len(q)
    wr = w[::-1] ?
    # We need conv of reversed q? Previously R = q_rev * w, slice n-1:2n-1.
    a = q[::-1]
    conv = fftconvolve(a, w, mode='full')
    return np.real(conv[n-1:2*n-1])
```

Define:
```
def full_convolve_real(a,b):
    n_full=len(a)+len(b)-1
    f=np.fft.rfft(a, n=next_fast_len(n_full))
    g=np.fft.rfft(b, n=...)
    return np.fft.irfft(f*g, n=n_full)[:n_full]
```

Derivative similarly:
```
def cross_shift(w, q):
    n=len(q); m=len(w) # 2n-1
    n_fft=power2(n+m-1)
    a=np.fft.rfft(q[::-1], n=n_fft)
    b=np.fft.rfft(w, n=n_fft)
    return np.fft.irfft(a*b, n=n_fft)[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Let q = softmax(θ). G(θ) = H(q(θ)), H = (1/β) log ∑_k exp(β c_k), c = full conv.
∂H/∂q_i = ∑_k w_k ∂c_k/∂q_i = 2∑_k w_k q_{k-i} = 2 d_i, where d_i =∑_k w_k q_{k-i}. Then ∂H/∂θ_i = ∑_j ∂H/∂q_j (q_j δ_{ij} - q_j q_i) = q_i (2d_i - 2∑_j d_j q_j). If we ignore constant 2. So g_i = q_i*(d_i - mean_d). Note d_i ≥0. mean_d = ∑ q_j d_j = ∑_k w_k ∑_j q_j q_{k-j} = ∑_k w_k c_k. So mean_d = weighted sum c. Then g_i = q_i(d_i - weighted_c_sum). This makes sum g_i=0. Update θ -= lr * g. Since g positive if coordinate participates more than average; reduce those. Good.

Algorithm single sequence optimization:
```
def optimize(q0, n, budget, beta_schedule, ...):
    q = q0 normalized
    best = q.copy(); best_score = score(q)
    for stage:
       beta = ...
       w0 = softmax(beta*c)
       iterations...
           c = conv(q,q)
       w = softmax(beta*c - beta*maxc) maybe
       d = correlate(w,q)
       r = d - np.sum(q*d)  # = d - weighted_c_sum
       # scale r: maybe divide by max(abs(r))
       for step in decreasing:
           q2 = q * np.exp(-step*r/scale)
           q2 = np.clip(q2,eps,inf); q2/=sum
           score2 or H2; if improve surrogate: accept
       if no improvement after many, reduce beta? etc.
```

Manual gradient descent with momentum might be easier to control time and can be annealed with random restarts. Let's consider doing manual:
```
def surrogate_grad(q,beta):
  c=conv(q,q)
  w=softmax(beta,c)
  d=correlate(w,q)
  r=d - np.dot(q,d)
  return r, surrogate_value
```
The surrogate objective on simplex: Actually H(θ) = logsumexp/beta. Gradient wrt θ = q * r (since r=d-mean). So for Riemannian mirror descent, update q <- q * exp(-lr * r) (because r is gradient / q? Manual algorithm with line search:
```
q = normalize
best...
for iter in range(maxiter):
  if time up break
  c=conv(q,q)
  val = surrogate(c,beta)
  w=softmax(c,beta)
  d=correlate(w,q)
  r=d - np.dot(q,d)
  scale = np.max(np.abs(r)) + 1e-300
  # Try steps from step_max maybe 0.8 down
  q2=None
  for step in [0.8,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
      f=np.exp(-step*r/scale)
      f=np.clip(f, 1e-300, 1e300)
      q2=q*f; q2=q2/sum
      val2=surrogate(conv(q2,q2), beta)  # maybe expensive
      if val2 < val - 1e-12: accept
  if no accept: perturb q randomly
```
But evaluating val2 with conv each step expensive (7 per iter). Could only check exact score occasionally. Objective function `fun(theta)`:
```
q=softmax(theta)
c=conv(q,q)
m=max(c)
z=beta*(c-m)
return m + log(sum(exp(z)))/beta
```
This is smooth beta log-sum-exp. For beta large, if c differences small, logsumexp ~m + log(count_active)/beta. Gradient stable.
`jac(theta)`:
```
q=softmax(theta)
c=conv(q,q)
w=exp(beta*(c-m))/sum
d=correlate(w,q)
g=q*(d - dot(q,d))
return g
```

LP implementation details and reliability:
```
def lp_improve(q, M=None, U=None, num_steps=10, t=0.05):
 n=len(q); S=q.sum(); if M None: c=conv; M=max(c)
 for step in range(num_steps):
   # normalize q to sum S? keep sum1
   c=conv(q,q); M=max(c)
   A = build_matrix(q)
   res=linprog(c=-ones? maximize => min -sum, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2n-1,M), bounds=(0,U))
   if success: g=res.x; g_sum=sum(g); if g_sum>0: g=g*(1/g_sum)
       q2=(1-t)*q + t*g; wait q sum1, g sum1. But g direction from LP maximizing sum under M; if M from q, g likely has convolution with q ≤M.
       score2=exact(q2); if score2<score: accept
```
Build matrix efficiently: A rows length 2n-1, columns n. We can build dense using vectorized indexing:
For each k, start=max(0,k-(n-1)), end=min(k,n-1), cols range start..end, values q[k-j] for j in cols. Since q changes each LP step, rebuild.
```
A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
for k in range(2*n-1):
    s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k); A[k,s:e+1]=q[k-e:k-s-1? reversed?]
```

We need robust step control. Use exact objective and surrogate. We can perform a "barrier" update:
```
def reject_once(q, beta):
 c=conv(q,q)
 w=softmax(beta*c)
 d=correlate(w,q)
 r=d - np.dot(q,d)
 # normalize r by its max abs
 scale = np.max(np.abs(r)) + 1e-30
 # propose several step sizes
 for step in [0.35, 0.175, 0.0875, 0.0437, 0.0219]:
   q2 = q * np.exp(-step * r / scale)
   q2 = np.maximum(q2, 1e-300); q2 /= q2.sum()
   # evaluate surrogate or exact
   if exact_score(q2) < exact_score(q): accept
 return q, improved
```
This uses exact score for each candidate (5 conv per iteration) expensive but for n=1000 ok? 5 conv. Could use surrogate for candidates and periodic exact. But exact max is non-smooth; accepting surrogate may increase exact. We can accept if surrogate improves and exact not monitored. - Compute r, scale.

Manual fallback:
```
def manual_grad_step(theta,n,beta,step):
  obj=LSJObjective(n,beta)
  q,c,val=...
  grad=obj.grad(theta)
  # normalize grad RMS
  gnorm=np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad))
  t=step/(gnorm+1e-30)
  theta2=theta - t*grad
  return theta2, val
```

```
import time
import numpy as np
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAS_SCIPY=True

    HAS_SCIPY=False

MAXN? 

def _fast_len(n):
    if n <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()

def _conv_full(a,b):
    nfull = len(a)+len(b)-1
    f = _fast_len(nfull)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, f)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, f)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A*B, f)
    return C[:nfull]

def _conv_self(q):
   return _conv_full(q,q)

def _corr_w_q(w,q):
    n=len(q)
    nfull = n+len(w)-1
    f=_fast_len(nfull)
    A=np.fft.rfft(q[::-1], f)
    B=np.fft.rfft(w, f)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*B, f)
    return C[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check `_corr` slice: `2*n-1` exclusive yields length n? start n-1, end 2n-1 length n. Yes. For n=1: start0 end1 length1. w length1, conv len1? nfull=1, f=1, C[0:1] good.
Need ensure `2*n-1` not `2*n`. For n=3 earlier end5; 2*n-1=5, good. Actually length=2n-1, exclusive end = start+n = n-1+n=2n-1. Good.

`_exact_score(q)`:
```
def exact_score(q):
    if q.size==0: return np.inf
    s=q.sum()
    if s<0.01: return np.inf
    c=_conv_self(q)
    return float(2.0*q.size*np.max(c)/(s*s))
```
For speed, use `np.max(c)`.

```
return e/e.sum(), t
```

```
class LSJObjective:
    def __init__(self,n,beta,cap=20.0): ...
    def _values(self,theta):
       q,t = softmax
       c=conv_self(q)
       maxc=c.max()
       z=self.beta*(c-maxc)
       # to avoid sum under? z<=0
       # if beta very large and z< -745 exp 0 fine
       logsum = np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
       val = self.n*(maxc + logsum/self.beta)
       return val, q, c, maxc, z, logsum
    def value(self,theta): return self._values(theta)[0]
    def grad(self,theta):
       val,q,c,maxc,z,logsum = self._values(theta)
       # weights w = exp(z)/sumexp. Need sumexp; in _values we didn't store. Store? Or compute exp again.
       w = np.exp(z)
       w /= w.sum()

       # theta derivative of u
       # t from softmax? We didn't return t. Need compute tanh(theta/cap). Could include in _values.
```

```
q=normalize; best_q=q; best_score=exact
step=0.2
no_improve=0
while time<deadline and no_improve<500:
   c=conv(q,q); maxc=max
   w=softmax_weights(c, beta*n)
   d=corr(w,q); r=d-np.dot(q,d)
   scale=np.max(np.abs(r))
   if scale<1e-30: break
   improved=False
   for trial in range(6):
      q2=q*np.exp(-step*r/scale)
      q2=np.maximum(q2,1e-300); q2/=q2.sum()
      score2=exact_score(q2)
      if score2 < best_score*(1-1e-12) or score2 < best_score - 1e-12:
          q=q2; best...
          improved=True
          step*=1.05
          break
      step*=0.5
   if not improved:
      no_improve+=1
      # perturb q
      noise=rng.normal(size=n, scale=0.01)
      q2=q*np.exp(noise); normalize
      score2=exact
      if score2<best: accept else q=best? revert
      if no_improve>...: break
   else no_improve=0
```

Let's include L-BFGS primary. To avoid double eval, we can use a custom simple gradient descent with combined grad:
```
def simple_optimize(theta, n, betas, time_budget):
  step=0.5
  for beta:
    for it in range(maxit):
       val, grad = fun_grad(theta)
       gnorm = norm(grad)
       if gnorm < 1e-12: break
       lr = step / gnorm
       theta2 = theta - lr*grad
       val2 = fun(theta2)
       if val2 < val:
          theta=theta2; step*=1.1
       else:
          step*=0.5; if step<1e-6: break
```
This calls fun+grad (two conv) and fun (one) per accepted step =3 conv. L-BFGS maybe similar. But simple GD may be slow. L-BFGS is better.

```
    # final polish on best_q with remaining

```

```
def make_theta(n, idx, rng):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   if idx ==0:
      s=2*x-1; amp=0.5
   elif idx==1:
      s=np.sin(2*np.pi*x); amp=0.7  # antisym? sin(2πx) with x=(i+0.5)/n antisym yes.
   elif idx==2:
      r=rng.normal(size=n); s=(r-r[::-1]); s-=s.mean(); amp=0.7
   elif idx==3:
      s=np.sin(2*np.pi*2*x) + 0.3*np.sin(2*np.pi*x); amp=0.6
   elif idx==4:
      # random weighted two modes
      m1=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//30))) etc
   elif idx==5: linear amp=0.8

   maxs=np.max(np.abs(s)); if maxs==0: u=ones else amp_eff=amp*0.99/maxs; u=1+amp_eff*s; u=np.maximum(u,1e-6); q=u/sum
   theta=np.log(q); theta-=theta.mean(); theta=np.clip(theta,-15,15)
```

```
def build_A(q):
 n=len(q); A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
 for k in range(2*n-1):
   s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(k,n-1)
   if s<=e:
      # fill using advanced indexing
      idx=np.arange(s,e+1)
      A[k, idx]=q[k-idx]
 return A
```
This loop 2n up to 2048, each vector length up to n. O(1M) Python vector operations; maybe 0.05s. `linprog` dense with A 2047x1024 and b 2047; HiGHS may take seconds.

```
def manual_optimize(q, deadline, n, max_iters=20000, betaA=50):
  theta=np.log(q); theta-=theta.mean(); step=0.5; no=0
  obj=LSJObjective(n,betaA*n,cap)
  best=copy; best_score=exact
  while time<deadline:
     val, grad = obj.value_grad? We only have value and grad separate; implement `value_grad` method.
     gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
     if gnorm<1e-10: break
     # use adaptive step normalized to gradient norm: theta2=theta-step*grad/gnorm? If step=1, change in θ O1.
     accepted=False
     for trial step in [step, step*0.5, ...]
        theta2=theta - trial*grad/gnorm
        val2=obj.value(theta2)
        if val2<val: accept; step=min(2,step*1.1); break
        else: step*=0.5
     if not accept:
       # random perturb

       no++
       if no>100: break
     # periodic exact update
     if it%50==0: q=softmax(theta); score=exact; update best
```

Let's code class methods:
```
class LSJObjective:
    def __init__(self,n,beta,cap=20.0):
       self.n=int(n); self.beta=float(beta); self.cap=float(cap)
       self.scale=float(n)*float(n)
    def _softmax(self,theta):
       t=np.tanh(theta/self.cap)
       u=self.cap*t
       # center u? subtract mean for stability, but value invariant
       u = u - np.mean(u)
       z = u - np.max(u)
       e = np.exp(z)
       q = e / np.sum(e)
       return q,t
    def fun_grad(self,theta):
       q,t=self._softmax(theta)
       c=_conv_self(q)
       maxc=float(np.max(c))
       z=self.beta*(c-maxc)
       # z max 0; if beta extremely large and c differences negative small? underflow ok
       ez=np.exp(z)
       s=float(np.sum(ez))
       logsum=np.log(s)
       val=self.scale*(maxc + logsum/self.beta)
       w=ez/s
       d=_corr_w_q(w,q)
       mean=float(np.dot(q,d))
       gu=q*(d-mean)
       dth=self.cap*(1.0 - t*t)
       grad=self.scale*gu*dth
       return val, grad
    def value(self,theta): return self.fun_grad(theta)[0]
    def grad(self,theta): return self.fun_grad(theta)[1]
```
Double conv in value/grad if called separately. For manual, use fun_grad. For minimize, separate. We can maybe name `objective` class with a cache of last theta to avoid recomputing when value and grad called consecutively. Let's implement a simple cache using `self._last_theta_list`? We can store the last theta array and the corresponding val, grad, and maybe a hash. But if SciPy calls value on same theta as previous grad, we can return. It often does: line search evaluates value at trial points, then at final point calls grad. It may call value and grad at the same point at end. Cache helps a bit.

```
    # store copy? If theta later modified? SciPy passes arrays; we can store a copy and a tuple of values? Comparing entire array costs.
    self._last_theta = theta.copy(); self._last_val=val; self._last_grad=grad
def fun_grad(self,theta):
    if self._last_theta is not None and theta.shape==self._last_theta.shape:
       # use bitwise? np.array_equal maybe okay.
       if np.array_equal(theta, self._last_theta):
          return self._last_val, self._last_grad
    compute... store
```

`polish(q, deadline, rng, max_iters=500)`:
```
best_q=q.copy(); best_score=exact_score(q)
step=0.1
no_improve=0
betaA = 100.0
while time<deadline and no_improve<100:
   n=len(q)
   c=_conv_self(q); maxc=max(c)
   # if best score already very low and no need? continue
   # weights for exact max: take top lags where c > maxc - 0.05*maxc maybe.
   tol = 0.05*maxc
   active = c > maxc - tol
   if not active.any(): active[np.argmax(c)]=True
   w = active.astype(float)/active.sum()
   d=_corr_w_q(w,q); r=d-np.dot(q,d)
   scale=np.max(np.abs(r))
   if scale<1e-20: break
   improved=False
   for _ in range(5):
      q2 = q*np.exp(-step*r/scale)
      q2 = np.maximum(q2,1e-300); q2/=q2.sum()
      score2 = exact_score(q2)
      if score2 < best_score*(1-1e-12) or score2 < best_score - 1e-12:
          q=q2; best_score=score2; best_q=q2.copy(); improved=True; step=min(1.0,step*1.1); break
      step *= 0.5
   if not improved:
      no_improve +=1
      # random perturbation
      eps=0.02 if no_improve<20 else 0.05
      noise = rng.normal(size=n)
      q2=q*np.exp(eps*noise)
      q2=np.maximum(q2,1e-300); q2/=q2.sum()
      score2=exact_score(q2)
      if score2<best_score:
         q=q2; best...; no_improve=0
      else:
         q=best_q.copy()
         if no_improve>50: break
   else:
      no_improve=0
```

Implementation:
```
def lp_polish(q, deadline, max_iters=6):
   best_q=q.copy(); best_score=exact_score(q)
   n=len(q)
   if n>1024: return best_q,best_score
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>deadline-5: break
      c=_conv_self(q); M=float(np.max(c))
      A=np.empty((2*n-1,n))
      # fill
      for k in range(2*n-1): ...
      try:
         res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
      except Exception: break
      if not res.success: break
      g=res.x; s=float(np.sum(g))
      if not finite or s<=0: break
      g=g/s
      improved=False
      for lam in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6]:
         q2=(1-lam)*q + lam*g
         q2=np.maximum(q2,0); q2=q2/q2.sum()
         sc=exact_score(q2)
         if sc<best_score*(1-1e-12) or sc<best_score-1e-12:
            best_q=q2; best_score=sc; q=q2; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
         break
   return best_q,best_score
```

Now, code for resampling:
```
def resample_nonnegative(q, m):
   q=np.asarray(q,dtype=float)
   q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
   s=q.sum()
   if s<=0 or q.size==0: return np.ones(m)
   q=q/s
   if q.size==m: return q
   old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,q.size)
   new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
   qn=np.interp(new, old, q)
   qn=np.maximum(qn,0)
   s=qn.sum()
   if s<=0: qn=np.ones(m)
   else: qn/=s
   return qn
```
Endpoint linear may produce zero at ends if q ends zero; fine.

`make_theta_from_q` uses log with eps.

```
if best_q is not None:
   q=resample_nonnegative(best_q,n); theta=make_theta_from_q(q); starts.append(theta)
# generate until num total:
idx=0
while len(starts)<num:
   theta=make_theta_kind(n, idx, rng); starts.append(theta); idx+=1
return starts
```

```
def manual_optimize(theta, n, deadline, max_iters=20000):
  best_q=softmax...; best_score=exact
  step=1.0
  no=0
  for A in [5,15,40,100]:
     obj=LSJObjective(n,A*n)
     for it in range(max_iters//4):
        if time>deadline: break
        val, grad = obj.fun_grad(theta)
        gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
        if gnorm<1e-12: break
        accepted=False
        s=step
        for _ in range(6):
            theta2=theta - s*grad/gnorm
            val2 = obj.value(theta2)
            if val2 < val*(1-1e-12) or val2 < val - 1e-12:
               theta=theta2; accepted=True; step=min(2.0,s*1.05); break
            s*=0.5
        if not accepted:
            # random kick
            theta = theta + rng.normal(0,0.05,n)? need rng passed.
            no+=1
            if no>200: break
        if it%100==0: q... update
```

`_softmax_q(theta, cap=50.0)` top-level? Class uses method; but for other uses, define standalone:
```
def _softmax_q(theta, cap=50.0):
    t=np.tanh(theta/cap)
    u=cap*t
    u = u - np.mean(u)
    z = u - np.max(u)
    e=np.exp(z)
    q=e/np.sum(e)
    return q
```
Class can call this? But needs t as well. Define `_softmax_pair(theta,cap)`. Use in class and elsewhere.

```
def _softmax_pair(theta, cap=50.0):
    t=np.tanh(theta/cap)
    u=cap*t
    u = u - u.mean()
    z=u-u.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    q=e/e.sum()
    return q,t
```

`_exact_score`:
```
def _exact_score(q):
    q=np.asarray(q,float)
    if q.size==0: return np.inf
    s=float(q.sum())
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    c=_conv_self(q)
    mc=float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0*q.size*mc/(s*s))
```
Maybe c all negative due roundoff? mc negative? Not. `max(0, mc)`.

`_make_theta_kind`:
```
def _make_theta_kind(n, kind, rng, cap=50.0):
   n=int(n)
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
   if kind==0: s=2*x-1; amp=0.6
   elif kind==1: s=np.sin(2*np.pi*x); amp=0.7
   elif kind==2: s=np.sin(2*np.pi*x); amp=0.9
   elif kind==3: r=rng.standard_normal(n); s=r - r[::-1]; s-=s.mean(); amp=0.7
   elif kind==4: s=np.sin(2*np.pi*x)+0.35*np.sin(4*np.pi*x); s-=s.mean(); amp=0.7
   elif kind==5: r=rng.standard_normal(n); s=r - r[::-1]; s-=s.mean(); amp=1.0
   elif kind==6: r=rng.standard_normal(n); s=r - r[::-1]; s-=s.mean(); exponent later
   elif kind==7: s=2*x-1; amp=0.8
   else: r=rng.standard_normal(n); s=r-r[::-1]; s-=s.mean(); amp=0.8
   s=np.asarray(s,float)
   if s.size != n: ...
   s = s - s.mean()
   maxs=float(np.max(np.abs(s))) if s.size else 0
   if maxs > 0:
       if kind==6:
           s_norm=s/maxs
           eta = 0.5 + 0.1*int(kind % 5) ??? use 0.7
           u=np.exp(0.7*s_norm)
       else:
           a=amp*0.99/maxs
           u=1.0 + a*s
   else: u=np.ones(n)
   u=np.maximum(u,1e-12)
   u=u/u.sum()
   theta=np.log(u)
   theta-=theta.mean()
   theta=np.clip(theta,-15.0,15.0)
   return theta
```
For kind 0 `s` max1, amp 0.6/0.99 => 0.606, positive. For kind 2 amp0.9 =>0.898, positive (min 0.102). For kind 8 linear amp0.8 =>0.808. Good.
For random, if maxs huge, a small. If random s has positive/negative, positive.
For exponent kind, u ratio e^{0.7}/e^{-0.7}=4.4; moderate. Good.

Could use `rng` global in `make_theta`; for kind not random, rng unused okay.

`_make_theta_from_q`:
```
def _make_theta_from_q(q, cap=50.0):
   q=np.asarray(q,float); q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum()
   if s<=0: q=np.ones(q.size)/q.size
   else: q=q/s
   q=np.maximum(q,1e-300)
   theta=np.log(q); theta-=theta.mean(); theta=np.clip(theta,-15,15); return theta
```

```
def _manual_optimize(theta,n,deadline,rng):
   best_q,best_score...
   current=theta.copy()
   betas_A=[5,20,60]
   for A in betas_A:
      obj=LSJObjective(n,A*n)
      step=1.0
      no=0
      for it in range(2000):
         if time>=deadline: break
         val,grad=obj.fun_grad(current)
         gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
         if not finite or gnorm<1e-14: break
         accept=False; s=step
         for _ in range(6):
            cand=current - s*grad/gnorm
            cand-=cand.mean()
            val2=obj.value(cand)
            if finite and val2 < val*(1-1e-12) or val2 < val-1e-12:
                current=cand; accept=True; step=min(2.0,s*1.1); break
            s*=0.5
         if not accept:
            no +=1
            if no>100: break
            kick = 0.05 if no<30 else 0.1
            current = current + rng.standard_normal(n)*kick
            current -= current.mean()
         if it % 100 == 0:
            q=_softmax_q(current); sc=_exact_score(q); update
   return best
```

Now, `_polish_exact` as earlier. Need use local exact. We'll include but not too aggressive.
```
def _polish_exact(q, deadline, rng):
    q=np.asarray(q,float).copy(); q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s<=0: fallback; q/=s
    best_q=q.copy(); best_score=_exact_score(q)
    n=q.size
    step=0.15
    no=0
    max_no = 80 if n<=2048 else 40
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5 and no < max_no:
       it +=1
       c=_conv_self(q)
       mc=float(np.max(c))
       if not finite or mc<=0: break
       tol = 0.02 * mc  # active window
       active = c >= mc - tol
       if not active.any(): active[int(np.argmax(c))]=True
       w=active.astype(float); w/=w.sum()
       d=_corr_w_q(w,q)
       r=d - float(np.dot(q,d))
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(r)))
       if not finite or scale <=1e-30: break
       improved=False
       s=step
       for _ in range(5):
           q2=q*np.exp(-s*r/scale)
           q2=np.maximum(q2,1e-300); ss=q2.sum(); if ss<=0: q2=q; break
           q2/=ss
           sc2=_exact_score(q2)
           if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score*(1 - 1e-13) or sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
               q=q2; best_score=sc2; best_q=q2.copy(); improved=True; step=min(1.0, s*1.1); break
           s*=0.5
       if not improved:
          no +=1
          kick=0.02 if no<20 else 0.05
          noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
          q2=q*np.exp(kick*noise)
          q2=np.maximum(q2,1e-300); q2/=q2.sum()
          sc2=_exact_score(q2)
          if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
              best_score=sc2; best_q=q2.copy(); q=q2.copy(); no=0
          else:
              q=best_q.copy()
          if no >= max_no: break
       else:
          no=0
       if it % 50 ==0: print
    return best_q,best_score
```
Active tol 0.02 mc may be too narrow; gradient can get stuck. Use 0.1? If active too wide, similar to β. Use `tol=0.05*mc`. Good.

This polish uses exact score for 5 candidates; for n large, could be expensive. Limit max_no. Fine.

```
def _build_conv_matrix(q):
   n=q.size; A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
   # Fill columns? We determined A[k,i]=q[k-i]
   # For each i, column i rows i..i+n-1 = q
   for i in range(n):
       A[i:i+n, i] = q
   return A
```

`_lp_polish`:
```
def _lp_polish(q, deadline, max_iter=4):
   if not HAVE_SCIPY_LINPROG: return q, _exact_score(q)
   n=q.size
   if n>1024: return q, _exact_score(q)
   best_q=q.copy(); best_score=_exact_score(q)
   A_cache? none
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
      c=_conv_self(q); M=float(np.max(c))*(1+1e-7)+1e-12
      try: A=_build_conv_matrix(q)
      except Exception: break
      try:
          cvec=-np.ones(n)
          bvec=np.full(2*n-1, M, dtype=float)
          bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
          res=scipy_linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
      except Exception:
          break
      if not getattr(res,'success',False): break
      g=np.asarray(res.x,float); s=float(g.sum())
      if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-12: break
      g=np.maximum(g,0); g/=g.sum()
      improved=False
      for lam in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6):
          q2=(1-lam)*q + lam*g
          q2=np.maximum(q2,0); ss=q2.sum()
          if ss<=0: continue
          q2/=ss
          sc2=_exact_score(q2)
          if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score*(1-1e-12) or sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
              best_score=sc2; best_q=q2.copy(); q=q2.copy(); improved=True; break
      if not improved: break
   return best_q,best_score
```
Potential: `linprog` for unbounded? Bounds provided. If M small and no feasible due numerical, fail.

Time: For n=1024 one LP may be slow. Only run if time remaining > 180 and best_score > 1.52. Use max_iter=3.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800.0, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_q=None; best_score=np.inf
    # parent length
    large_n=4096
    try:
       parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float)
       if parent.ndim==1 and parent.size>=2048:
          large_n=int(parent.size)
          if large_n>8192: large_n=8192
    except Exception: pass
    # plans
    plans=[]
    plans.append((512,1))
    plans.append((1024,2))
    plans.append((2048,1 if large_n>2048 else 2))
    plans.append((large_n,2))
    # avoid duplicate n: sort unique, combine starts
    unique={}
    for n,s in plans:
       unique[n]=unique.get(n,0)+s
    n_list=sorted(unique.keys())
    # For time, process increasing
    remaining_plans=list(n_list)
    for n in n_list:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       starts=unique[n]
       # If n too large and no best? still.
       # budget for this n: maybe if n is first, allow 300s; but just use time check.
       for idx in range(starts):
          if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30: break
          # choose initial
          if best_q is not None and idx == 0:
             try:
                qinit=_resample_nonnegative(best_q, n)
                theta=_make_theta_from_q(qinit)
             except Exception:
                theta=_make_theta_kind(n, 0, rng)
          else:
             kind = idx
             theta=_make_theta_kind(n, kind, rng)
          q, score = _run_start(theta, n, deadline, rng)
          if score < best_score:
             best_score=score; best_q=q
          print("n=%d start=%d score=%.9f" % (n, idx, score))
       # after each n, maybe LP if n<=1024 and best from this n? We can LP best_q if best_q.size<=1024.
       if best_q is not None and best_q.size <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120 and best_score > 1.5027:
           try:
              q2, sc2 = _lp_polish(best_q.copy(), deadline, max_iter=3)
              if sc2 < best_score:
                  best_score=sc2; best_q=q2
              print("LP score=%.9f" % sc2)
           except Exception as e: print(e)
    # final polish
    if best_q is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
       try:
          q2, sc2 = _polish_exact(best_q.copy(), deadline, rng)
          if sc2 < best_score:
             best_score=sc2; best_q=q2
       except Exception as e: print(e)
    if best_q is None:
       best_q = np.ones(256)/256.0
    # ensure list floats
    out=[float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_q,dtype=float)]
    return out
```

```
class LSJObjective:
    def __init__(...):
       self._last_theta=None; self._last_result=None
    def _check_cache(self,theta):
       if self._last_theta is None or theta.shape != self._last_theta.shape: return None
       if np.array_equal(theta, self._last_theta): return self._last_result
       return None
```

```python
"""
Heuristic low-peak positive convolution optimizer.

The program searches for a non-negative sequence q with objective
    2*n*max(conv(q,q)) / sum(q)^2
smaller than 1.5027.  Since the objective is scale invariant, the search
maintains sum(q)=1.  It uses a smooth log-sum-exp surrogate of the maximum
convolution value, optimized in log-space by L-BFGS-B when SciPy is available,
with a simple adaptive gradient fallback.  Several sequence lengths and
anti-symmetric/trig/random initializations are tried, followed by exact
subgradient polishing and, for moderate lengths, an LP direction step inspired
by the previous state of the art.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY_MINIMIZE = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_MINIMIZE = False

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as scipy_linprog
    HAVE_SCIPY_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_LINPROG = False


def _fast_len(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(n) - 1).bit_length()


def _conv_full(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    nfull = int(a.size + b.size - 1)
    if nfull <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    s = _fast_len(nfull)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, s)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, s)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, s)
    return out[:nfull]


def _conv_self(q):
    return _conv_full(q, q)


def _corr_w_q(w, q):
    """Return d_i = sum_k w_k q_{k-i}, where q index is valid only in [0,n-1]."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(q.size)
    nfull = int(n + w.size - 1)
    s = _fast_len(nfull)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(q[::-1], s)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, s)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fq * fw, s)
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _softmax_pair(theta, cap=50.0):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.tanh(theta / cap)
    u = cap * t
    u = u - u.mean()
    z = u - u.max()
    e = np.exp(z)
    q = e / e.sum()
    return q, t


def _softmax_q(theta, cap=50.0):
    q, _ = _softmax_pair(theta, cap)
    return q


def _exact_score(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_self(q)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * q.size * mc / (s * s))


def _resample_nonnegative(q, m):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if q.size == 0 or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    q = q / s
    if q.size == m:
        return q.copy()
    if q.size == 1:
        return np.full(m, q.sum() / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, q.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    qn = np.interp(new, old, q)
    qn = np.maximum(qn, 0.0)
    s = float(qn.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return qn / s


def _make_theta_from_q(q, cap=50.0):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if q.size == 0 or s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(q.size, dtype=np.float64)
    q = q / s
    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
    theta = np.log(q)
    theta -= theta.mean()
    return np.clip(theta, -15.0, 15.0)


def _make_theta_kind(n, kind, rng, cap=50.0):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    if kind == 0:
        s = 2.0 * x - 1.0
        amp = 0.60
    elif kind == 1:
        s = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        amp = 0.70
    elif kind == 2:
        s = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        amp = 0.90
    elif kind == 3:
        r = rng.standard_normal(n)
        s = r - r[::-1]
        s -= s.mean()
        amp = 0.70
    elif kind == 4:
        s = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.35 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
        s -= s.mean()
        amp = 0.70
    elif kind == 5:
        r = rng.standard_normal(n)
        s = r - r[::-1]
        s -= s.mean()
        amp = 1.00
    elif kind == 6:
        r = rng.standard_normal(n)
        s = r - r[::-1]
        s -= s.mean()
        amp = 0.0  # exponential mode
    elif kind == 7:
        s = 2.0 * x - 1.0
        amp = 0.80
    else:
        r = rng.standard_normal(n)
        s = r - r[::-1]
        s -= s.mean()
        amp = 0.80

    s = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if s.size != n:
        s = np.random.default_rng(0).standard_normal(n)
    s = s - s.mean()
    maxs = float(np.max(np.abs(s))) if s.size else 0.0

    if maxs > 0.0:
        if kind == 6:
            u = np.exp(0.70 * s / maxs)
        else:
            a = amp * 0.99 / maxs
            u = 1.0 + a * s
    else:
        u = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.maximum(u, 1e-12)
    u = u / u.sum()
    theta = np.log(u)
    theta -= theta.mean()
    return np.clip(theta, -15.0, 15.0)


class LSJObjective:
    def __init__(self, n, beta, cap=50.0):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.beta = float(beta)
        self.cap = float(cap)
        self.scale = float(self.n) * float(self.n)
        self._last_theta = None
        self._last_result = None

    def _cache_get(self, theta):
        if self._last_theta is None:
            return None
        if theta.shape != self._last_theta.shape:
            return None
        if np.array_equal(theta, self._last_theta):
            return self._last_result
        return None

    def _cache_set(self, theta, val, grad):
        self._last_theta = theta.copy()
        self._last_result = (float(val), grad.copy())

    def fun_grad(self, theta):
        theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
        cached = self._cache_get(theta)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached

        q, t = _softmax_pair(theta, self.cap)
        c = _conv_self(q)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        z = self.beta * (c - maxc)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        s = float(ez.sum())
        logsum = float(np.log(s)) if s > 0.0 else 0.0
        val = self.scale * (maxc + logsum / self.beta)

        w = ez / s
        d = _corr_w_q(w, q)
        mean = float(np.dot(q, d))
        gu = q * (d - mean)
        dtheta_u = self.cap * (1.0 - t * t)
        grad = self.scale * gu * dtheta_u

        self._cache_set(theta, val, grad)
        return val, grad

    def value(self, theta):
        val, _ = self.fun_grad(theta)
        return val

    def grad(self, theta):
        _, grad = self.fun_grad(theta)
        return grad


def _manual_optimize(theta, n, deadline, rng, verbose=False):
    n = int(n)
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    theta -= theta.mean()
    q = _softmax_q(theta)
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(q)
    current = theta.copy()

    beta_ases = (5.0, 20.0, 60.0, 120.0)
    step = 0.8
    no_improve = 0

    for a in beta_ases:
        obj = LSJObjective(n, a * float(n), cap=50.0)
        for it in range(2500):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            val, grad = obj.fun_grad(current)
            gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
                break

            accepted = False
            s = step
            for _ in range(7):
                cand = current - (s / gnorm) * grad
                cand -= cand.mean()
                cand = np.clip(cand, -200.0, 200.0)
                try:
                    val2 = obj.value(cand)
                except Exception:
                    val2 = np.inf
                if np.isfinite(val2) and (val2 < val * (1.0 - 1e-12) or val2 < val - 1e-12):
                    current = cand
                    accepted = True
                    step = min(1.6, s * 1.08)
                    no_improve = 0
                    break
                s *= 0.5

            if not accepted:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 120:
                    break
                kick = 0.04 if no_improve < 30 else 0.10
                current = current + kick * rng.standard_normal(n)
                current -= current.mean()
                current = np.clip(current, -200.0, 200.0)

            if verbose and (it == 0 or it % 200 == 0):
                qq = _softmax_q(current)
                ss = _exact_score(qq)
                if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_score:
                    best_score = ss
                    best_q = qq.copy()

    qq = _softmax_q(current)
    ss = _exact_score(qq)
    if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_score:
        best_score = ss
        best_q = qq.copy()

    return best_q, best_score


def _run_start(theta, n, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    theta -= theta.mean()
    theta = np.clip(theta, -200.0, 200.0)

    q0 = _softmax_q(theta)
    best_q = q0.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(q0)
    print("start n=%d initial score=%.9f" % (n, best_score))

    if not HAVE_SCIPY_MINIMIZE:
        return _manual_optimize(theta, n, deadline, rng, verbose=False)

    beta_ases = (5.0, 15.0, 40.0, 100.0)

    if n <= 1024:
        base_maxiter = 250
    elif n <= 2500:
        base_maxiter = 190
    else:
        base_maxiter = 130

    current = theta.copy()
    for a in beta_ases:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        remaining = max(0.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic()))
        if remaining < 8.0:
            break

        maxiter = base_maxiter
        if remaining < 240.0:
            maxiter = int(maxiter * remaining / 240.0)
        maxiter = int(max(35, min(base_maxiter, maxiter)))

        beta = float(a) * float(n)
        obj = LSJObjective(n, beta, cap=50.0)

        q_cur = _softmax_q(current)
        sc_cur = _exact_score(q_cur)
        val_cur = np.inf
        try:
            val_cur, _ = obj.fun_grad(current)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            options = {
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxfun": max(100, int(maxiter * 15)),
                "maxls": 20,
                "maxcor": 20,
            }
            res = scipy_minimize(
                obj.value,
                current.copy(),
                jac=obj.grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=options,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print("L-BFGS error n=%d beta_a=%g: %s" % (n, a, exc))
            res = None

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            try:
                xr = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                if xr.shape == current.shape and np.all(np.isfinite(xr)):
                    theta_res = xr
                    theta_res -= theta_res.mean()
                    theta_res = np.clip(theta_res, -200.0, 200.0)
                    q_res = _softmax_q(theta_res)
                    sc_res = _exact_score(q_res)
                    val_res = getattr(res, "fun", np.inf)
                    if not np.isfinite(val_res):
                        try:
                            val_res, _ = obj.fun_grad(theta_res)
                        except Exception:
                            val_res = np.inf

                    use = False
                    if np.isfinite(val_res) and np.isfinite(val_cur) and val_res <= val_cur * (1.0 + 1e-12):
                        use = True
                    if np.isfinite(sc_res) and np.isfinite(sc_cur) and sc_res < sc_cur:
                        use = True

                    if use:
                        current = theta_res
                    if np.isfinite(sc_res) and sc_res < best_score:
                        best_score = float(sc_res)
                        best_q = q_res.copy()
                        print("improved n=%d beta_a=%g score=%.9f" % (n, a, best_score))
            except Exception as exc:
                print("stage eval error: %s" % (exc,))

    qfin = _softmax_q(current)
    sfin = _exact_score(qfin)
    if np.isfinite(sfin) and sfin < best_score:
        best_q = qfin.copy()
        best_score = float(sfin)

    return best_q, best_score


def _build_conv_matrix(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(q.size)
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        A[i:i + n, i] = q
    return A


def _lp_polish(q, deadline, max_iter=3):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY_LINPROG:
        return q.copy(), _exact_score(q)

    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        q = np.ones_like(q) / max(1, q.size)
        s = float(q.sum())
    q = q / s

    n = int(q.size)
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(q)

    if n > 900:
        return best_q, best_score

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 6.0:
            break
        c = _conv_self(q)
        M = float(np.max(c)) * (1.0 + 1e-7) + 1e-12
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        try:
            A = _build_conv_matrix(q)
        except Exception:
            break

        try:
            cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
            bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
            res = scipy_linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print("LP error: %s" % (exc,))
            break

        if not getattr(res, "success", False):
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(gs) or gs <= 1e-12:
            break
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        g = g / g.sum()

        improved = False
        for lam in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60):
            q2 = (1.0 - lam) * q + lam * g
            q2 = np.maximum(q2, 0.0)
            ss = float(q2.sum())
            if ss <= 0.0:
                continue
            q2 = q2 / ss
            sc2 = _exact_score(q2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and (sc2 < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12) or sc2 < best_score - 1e-12):
                best_score = float(sc2)
                best_q = q2.copy()
                q = q2.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    return best_q, best_score


def _polish_exact(q, deadline, rng):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        q = np.ones_like(q) / max(1, q.size)
        s = float(q.sum())
    q = q / s

    n = int(q.size)
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(q)

    step = 0.18
    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 80 if n <= 2048 else 45
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 3.0 and no_improve < max_no:
        it += 1
        c = _conv_self(q)
        mc = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc <= 0.0:
            break

        tol = 0.05 * mc
        active = (c >= mc - tol)
        if not np.any(active):
            active[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
        w = active.astype(np.float64)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / ws

        d = _corr_w_q(w, q)
        r = d - float(np.dot(q, d))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(r)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-30:
            break

        improved = False
        s = step
        for _ in range(5):
            q2 = q * np.exp(-s * r / scale)
            q2 = np.maximum(q2, 1e-300)
            ss = float(q2.sum())
            if ss <= 0.0:
                q2 = q.copy()
                break
            q2 = q2 / ss
            sc2 = _exact_score(q2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and (sc2 < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-13) or sc2 < best_score - 1e-13):
                best_score = float(sc2)
                best_q = q2.copy()
                q = q2.copy()
                improved = True
                step = min(1.0, s * 1.12)
                no_improve = 0
                break
            s *= 0.5

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            kick = 0.02 if no_improve < 20 else 0.05
            noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
            q2 = q * np.exp(kick * noise)
            q2 = np.maximum(q2, 1e-300)
            ss = float(q2.sum())
            if ss > 0.0:
                q2 = q2 / ss
                sc2 = _exact_score(q2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc2)
                    best_q = q2.copy()
                    q = q2.copy()
                    no_improve = 0

        if it % 50 == 0:
            print("polish score=%.9f" % best_score)

    return best_q, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800.0, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    best_q = None
    best_score = np.inf

    large_n = 4096
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size >= 2048:
            large_n = int(parent.size)
            if large_n > 8192:
                large_n = 8192
    except Exception:
        pass

    plan_counts = {}
    plan_counts[512] = plan_counts.get(512, 0) + 1
    plan_counts[1024] = plan_counts.get(1024, 0) + 2
    if large_n <= 2048:
        plan_counts[2048] = plan_counts.get(2048, 0) + 2
    else:
        plan_counts[2048] = plan_counts.get(2048, 0) + 1
    plan_counts[large_n] = plan_counts.get(large_n, 0) + 2

    n_order = sorted(plan_counts.keys())
    task_list = []
    for n in n_order:
        for _ in range(plan_counts[n]):
            task_list.append(n)

    total_tasks = len(task_list)
    task_idx = 0

    for n in n_order:
        for start_idx in range(plan_counts[n]):
            task_idx += 1
            rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
            if rem < 25.0:
                break

            remaining_tasks = total_tasks - task_idx + 1
            alloc = rem / float(max(1, remaining_tasks))
            start_deadline = t0 + 0.0
            cur = time.monotonic()
            start_deadline = cur + min(max(30.0, alloc), rem)
            if start_deadline > deadline:
                start_deadline = deadline

            if best_q is not None and start_idx == 0:
                try:
                    qinit = _resample_nonnegative(best_q, n)
                    theta = _make_theta_from_q(qinit)
                except Exception:
                    theta = _make_theta_kind(n, start_idx + 8, rng)
            else:
                theta = _make_theta_kind(n, start_idx + 2, rng)

            try:
                q, score = _run_start(theta, n, start_deadline, rng)
                if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                    best_score = float(score)
                    best_q = q.copy()
                print("done n=%d start=%d score=%.9f" % (n, start_idx, score))
            except Exception as exc:
                print("run_start failure n=%d: %s" % (n, exc))

        if (
            best_q is not None
            and best_q.size <= 900
            and best_score > 1.5027
            and time.monotonic() < deadline - 240.0
        ):
            lp_dead = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 180.0)
            try:
                q2, score2 = _lp_polish(best_q.copy(), lp_dead, max_iter=3)
                if np.isfinite(score2) and score2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(score2)
                    best_q = q2.copy()
                print("after LP score=%.9f" % best_score)
            except Exception as exc:
                print("LP failure: %s" % (exc,))

    if best_q is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        try:
            q2, score2 = _polish_exact(best_q.copy(), deadline, rng)
            if np.isfinite(score2) and score2 < best_score:
                best_score = float(score2)
                best_q = q2.copy()
            print("after polish score=%.9f" % best_score)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("polish failure: %s" % (exc,))

    if best_q is None:
        best_q = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_q, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    out_arr = np.maximum(out_arr, 0.0)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        out_arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / s

    return [float(x) for x in out_arr]
```
